 … RESPONDENTS

THIS WRIT PETITION IS FILED UNDER ARTICLES
226 AND 227 OF THE CONSTITUTION OF INDIA,
PRAYING TO DECLARE THAT SECTIONS 20(2)(a-iv)
AND (a-v) OF THE KARNATAKA CO-OPERATIVE
SOCIETIES ACT, 1959 ARE ULTRA VIRES OF THE
CONSTITUTION OF INDIA ETC.
 THIS PETITION COMING ON FOR ORDERS THIS
DAY, THE COURT MADE THE FOLLOWING:-
ORDER
Heard Sri B. Ravindra Prasad, learned Advocate
for the petitioners, Smt. Niloufer Akbar, learned AGA
for the respondent-State and Shri G.V. Shashikumar,
learned Advocate for respondent No.2.
2. Sri B. Ravindra Prasad, learned Advocate
submits that elections have already been conducted,
therefore, this petition is rendered infructuous and

331
accordingly prayed for its dismissal.
He undertakes to file a memo to this effect.
Once memo is received, Registry shall keep the same
in record.
3. In the circumstances, the petition is accordingly
dismissed as having become infructuous.
No costs.
 Sd/-
